## OPENING
Bed beneath you. Cup beside it. A book face-down on the floor.

The door has no handle on this side. A narrow slit sits at eye height.

Your mouth tastes stale. Your skin feels worse.

You are in a small locked room. Nothing is happening yet.

## Turn 1
> open the door

The cell remains your confined space, the door a stubborn barrier. The cup, book, and bed remain your solitary companions. The door, devoid of a handle on this side, refuses your unspoken plea, its silence an echo of your isolation. Your skin feels unclean, a constant reminder of your surroundings, while your words come out thin and broken, a testament to your desperation. The door, for now, remains closed, the air heavy with the weight of your solitude.

## Turn 2
> kick the door

The door, devoid of a handle on your side, remains unyielding. Your foot meets resistance as you attempt to kick, the impact echoing off the unresponsive wood. The room continues to surround you, a mute witness to your efforts. The bed, the cup, the book all remain untouched, the cup's rim gathering the remnants of your breath. Your skin, unclean from the night's rest, feels noticeably gritty against the rough fabric of your clothing. Your voice, hoarse from the dry air, barely manages a whisper as you exhale.

## Turn 3
> look for a window

As the first light of morning seeps through the narrow slit, you find yourself still in the cell, the bed beneath you offering no escape. The cup beside it, untouched, and a book face-down on the floor, its pages silent and unyielding. Your attempt to locate a window goes unfulfilled, as the door, devoid of a handle on your side, remains stubbornly closed. A lingering taste of staleness fills your mouth, and your skin, unclean, brushes against the worn cell walls. The door, the book, the cup, and the slit - these are your only companions in this solitary confinement.

## Turn 4
> climb through the slit

In the dimly lit cell, the slit at eye level, previously unnoticed, now becomes your focus. Your fingers find purchase on the rough edges, and with a determined effort, you climb through, the cool air of the unknown replacing the stale air of the cell. The sensation of unclean skin brushes against your consciousness, a stark reminder of your surroundings. The words you speak, though familiar, sound thin and broken in this new environment.

## Turn 5
> wait

The observation slit in the door, previously sealed, swings open, revealing a figure on the other side. Their words, mostly incomprehensible, are punctuated by gestures that echo a single, insistent message: retreat. The cell, with its familiar furnishings of bed, cup, book, and door, remains silent, save for the scratching of your own breath against the damp air. Your skin, long unused to the touch of cleanliness, feels unclean enough to notice, and your voice, rusty from disuse, comes out thin and broken. You wait, the cell holding its quiet as the figure outside continues their mute plea for distance.

## Turn 6
> wait

The cell keeps its quiet, the air heavy with anticipation. The observation slit in the door remains open, and the large, silent figure on the other side continues to wait, their gestures urging you to move back. Your body, unyielding, stands firm, and your words come out thin and broken. The cell's silence is deafening, the only sound the soft patter of rain against the cell walls. The unclean feel of your skin is a constant reminder of your surroundings, a stark contrast to the crisp, cool rain outside. Time passes, and the figure beyond the slit persists in their wait, their eyes never wavering from the door. You wait, too, the tension in the air palpable, as if you were both caught in a holding pattern, neither of you ready to break the silence.

## Turn 9
> escape

The air, already thick with tension, grows denser as a second figure steps into view, larger and more imposing than the first. The narrow slit in the heavy door, your only window to the corridor, reveals the silence of anticipation. The sound of footsteps echoes, a drumbeat of the approaching encounter. The first guard, having given up on further gestures, calls out, a signal for the larger figure to approach, ready to enter whether you yield or not. Your skin prickles with the weight of uncleanliness, and your voice, though you try to speak, comes out thin and broken. The bed, the cup, the book remain untouched, a stark reminder of the world beyond the door, for now, out of reach.

> flee down the corridor

## Turn 11
> break free

In the dimly lit washroom, a basin catches your eye. The staff, one large and silent, the other younger and tense, guide you towards it. The basin, cold and unyielding, seems to mock your attempts for freedom as they prepare to wash you. Yet, despite your efforts to break free, your bindings hold firm and your words come out thin and broken. The staff watch, their expressions unchanging, as the water remains stubbornly unwashed. The air is heavy with unease, a lingering tension that hangs between you and your captors. The routine procedure continues, a stark reminder of your confinement.

## Turn 14
> wait

In the dimly lit mess room, the air heavy with expectation, you find yourself confronted with a narrow table, a bowl of warm, steaming food, and a spoon. The smell of cooked meals wafts through the room, a stark contrast to the sterile washroom you've just left.

You wait, your hands bound, your heart pounding in your chest. The silence is thick, punctuated only by the faint sounds of clinking utensils and muffled voices from other parts of the facility.

Your resistance is futile as you're washed again, the air around you suddenly smelling less like you, the scent of soap and disinfectant replacing the familiar musk of your own body. Your skin, immediately, unmistakably cleaner.

The staff, a large, silent figure and a younger, tense individual, are still watching you, their gazes unwavering. The younger one glances at the bowl, then back at you, a hint of tension in her shoulders. Ruan

## Turn 15
> wait

In the dimly lit mess, the air heavy with anticipation, the silence is broken only by the soft hum of nearby conversations and the distant clanking of metal against metal. You, a lone figure, remain seated, your hands bound, the bowl before you untouched. The staff members, a pair of imposing figures, their gazes steady, continue to wait, their expectations clear: eat. The warmth emanating from the bowl, a contrast to the cold that lingers around you, serves as a reminder of the meal that awaits. Ruan, a presence that seems both lucid and fractured, watches you from afar, his eyes reflecting a mix of concern and curiosity. The meal, left untouched, sits as a physical barrier between you and the staff, a test of wills in this strange, institutional setting.

## Turn 16
> sleep

The air thickens with the scent of cooked food, a stark contrast to the sterile washroom you've just left. The bowl from before remains, now filled with a steaming concoction that stirs a gnawing hunger. The staff members, one large and silent, the other younger and tense, continue to watch you, their expectation palpable. Ruan, lucid and fractured, also gazes at you, mirroring the staff's anticipation. The silence grows heavier as the minutes tick by, a testament to the unresolved tension that lingers. Despite your efforts to resist, unease seeps into your ribs, and your hands, bound or not, feel the weight of their immobility. The words you utter are thin and broken, a mere echo of your unspoken resistance. The staff continue to wait, their eyes never wavering, the bowl of food still untouched.

The bowl from before remains, now filled with a steaming concoction that stirs a gnawing hunger. The staff members, one large and silent, the other younger and tense, continue to watch you, their expectation palpable. Ruan, lucid and fractured, also gazes at you, mirroring the staff's anticipation. The silence grows heavier as the minutes tick by, a testament to the unresolved tension that lingers. Despite your efforts to resist, unease seeps into your ribs, and your hands, bound or not, feel the weight of their immobility. The words you utter are thin and broken, a mere echo of your unspoken resistance. The staff continue to wait, their eyes never wavering, the bowl of food still untouched.

## Turn 17
> wait

In the dimly lit cell, you find yourself returned, the room much as you left it. The bed beneath you, the cup beside it, and the book face-down on the floor remain unchanged, save for a slight shift in their positions. The door, devoid of a handle on this side, continues its silent vigil. A narrow slit, eye-level, offers a glimpse of the corridor outside. A new presence fills the space, Ruan, a fellow subject, watching you silently. The air is heavy with the weight of unspoken words, and a dull tiredness sits behind your eyes. The unease under your ribs persists, and words come out thin and broken. As the day winds toward sleep, a cup of food appears on the floor, easing the gnawing hunger that had lingered. Rest, it seems, is expected.

--- J. NARRATOR OUTPUT ---
The cell remains as your prison, the echoes of the previous day fading into the quiet. A new day dawns, the light seeping through the barred window, casting long shadows across the room. The institution will collect you more formally today, a reminder of your confinement. You wait, your body sluggish from sleep, your mind still groggy. A grand plan to stay awake unfolds, only to be thwarted by the pull of slumber. You awaken later, your face pressed against the cold, unyielding wall. Unease sits under your ribs, words coming out thin and broken. The day stretches out before you, the institution's formal collection looming.

## Turn 21
> wait

In the morning, the collection becomes more formal. You're guided from the cell into a plain corridor, where the same bolted door echoes the one from your cell. The institution moves at its pace, hands on your arms. You wait, your breath matching the rhythm of footsteps, the cell's quiet lingering. The air is thick with unease, words coming out thin and broken. Iven, the gentle believer, remains by your side. The staff members, one large, silent, practical, the other younger, tense, rule-bound, continue to escort you toward the next room. The tension remains unresolved, they expect compliance with the escort. The cell, for now, keeps its quiet.

## Turn 23
> wait

In the sterile, well-lit confines of the interview room, the air is thick with tension as Sovan, a figure clad in a precisely fitted collar, sits across from you. The table between you is adorned with images and objects, arranged as if for a lesson. Your heart pounds in your chest, a physical response to the looming questions that hang heavy in the air. The room seems to hum with the quiet anticipation of the staff members present, their faces a mix of controlled professionalism and unwavering gaze. The room, previously dim, now gleams with a brighter light, casting long shadows across the cold floor. A picture of water and low houses sits among the other objects, a question perhaps, a clue to a home that seems distant and unreachable. The words you speak feel thin and broken, as if they lack the weight to carry the truth.

In the sterile, well-lit confines of the interview room, the air is thick with tension as Sovan, a figure clad in a precisely fitted collar, sits across from you. The table between you is adorned with images and objects, arranged as if for a lesson. Your heart pounds in your chest, a physical response to the looming questions that hang heavy in the air. The room seems to hum with the quiet anticipation of the staff members present, their faces a mix of controlled professionalism and unwavering gaze. The room, previously dim, now gleams with a brighter light, casting long shadows across the cold floor. A picture of water and low houses sits among the other objects, a question perhaps, a clue to a home that seems distant and unreachable. The words you speak feel thin and broken, as if they lack the weight to carry the truth.

> no

In the confines of the facility, the air chills, echoing the wooden floor beneath your feet, evoking a sense of childhood. The room remains silent, the tension unresolved, the questions still hanging in the air.

## Turn 26
> wait

In the dimly lit interview room, the questioning shifts. The staff member who seems controlled, collared, professionally kind, presents a bowl and bread. Your response is a silence, a wait that hangs heavy in the air. The room remains still, the other figures - the large, silent, practical staff member and Nessa, the abrasive sceptic - watch expectantly. The absence of an answer seems to unnerve them, as they continue their line of inquiry.

The conversation takes a somber turn as they ask about the memories of your death. You share nothing, and instead, the staff member who seems controlled describes a series of vivid, yet disturbing recollections: darkness, waking, warmth, pain, voices, light. The words come out thin and broken, echoing the emptiness of your response.

Unease settles under your ribs as you struggle to find the right words, the right memories to share. The staff members look at you with a mix of concern and curiosity, waiting for an explanation

## Turn 28
> wait

In the sterile facility, the atmosphere thickens with anticipation. The controlled staff member, clad in a fitted collar, leans forward, their gaze fixed on you. The silent, practical one, their presence a constant, merely nods. Nessa, the abrasive sceptic, maintains a stoic demeanor, her skepticism palpable.

The room falls silent as the controlled staff member asks, "Hands, nets, repetitive work. Yours?" The question hangs in the air, a stark reminder of the facility's routine and your place within it.

The warmth and sunlight, the clean bedding, food, and running water - all seemingly memories or fabrications - linger in your mind, a stark contrast to the cold, sterile environment that surrounds you. Your hands, clenched tightly, remain still, a silent testament to your inability to provide the answers they seek.

A feeling of unease settles under your ribs, and your words come out

In the facility, the atmosphere thickens as you find yourself ensconced within the interview room. The air is heavy with a shared tension, the silence a palpable entity that hangs between you, Sovan, Dom, and Nessa. The room's occupants - a staff member who exudes control, another who is practical and large, and Nessa, the abrasive sceptic - all maintain a watchful gaze, their immediate objectives clear: Sovan and the large staff member are there to complete procedures, while Nessa seeks to learn about your transfer.

The room remains unchanged, a cold and sterile space with its wooden floor and table adorned with images and objects, as if a lesson were about to unfold. The memory of cold air and the sensation of wood under your feet lingers, a reminder of the recent events that have brought you here.

As the minutes tick by, the room seems to close in as they wait, and you find yourself grappling with the question they pose: What were you afraid
